 tla[4]*tla_rate[5] + tlb[4]*tlb_rate[5],\n    tla[5]*tla_rate[6] + tlb[5]*tlb_rate[6],\n    tla[6]*tla_rate[7] + tlb[6]*tlb_rate[7],\n]\n\nebt = [ebit[i]-interest[i] for i in range(8)]\ntax = [max(0, exit_ev - exit_debt + exit_cash\n\nmgmt_pool = exit_equity_proceeds * 0.10\nsponsor_proceeds = exit_equity_proceeds - mgmt_pool\nsponsor_equity = 208.3\n\nirr = (sponsor_proceeds / sponsor_equity)**(1/5) - 1\nmoic = sponsor_proceeds / sponsor_equity\n\nprint(f\"Exit EBITDA: {exit_ebitda:.1f}\")\nprint(f\"Exit EV: {exit_ev:.1f}\")\nprint(f\"Exit Debt: {exit_debt:.1f}\")\nprint(f\"Exit Equity Proceeds: {exit_equity_proceeds:.1f}\")\nprint(f\"Mgmt Pool: {mgmt_pool:.1f}\")\nprint(f\"Sponsor Proceeds: {sponsor_proceeds:.1f}\")\nprint(f\"Sponsor Equity: {sponsor_equity:.1f}\")\nprint(f\"IRR: {irr:.2%}\")\nprint(f\"MOIC: {moic:.2f}x\")\n\nprint(\"\\nAnnual FCF:\")\nfor i in range(8):\n    print(f\"  {years[i]}: {fcf[i]:.1f}\")\n\n#
